- **seeing**: Use when a vivid demonstration (an animation, a movie) is offered as proof. Seeing is not proving: the evidence a rendering carries is the CLAIM's, never the frame count's — vividness is orthogonal to proof. Assume-nothing is the neutral prior (not disbelief). Belief flips only on a passing test, and against a neutral prior that flip IS surprise. The door is open; the key is a test, not a movie. ([erpax/erpax](https://github.com/erpax/erpax/tree/HEAD/src/seeing) · ★2)
